# Sources and licences

Every ingested row must point at a `source` registered here. Append a row when
you add a source; never rewrite the file. No number is stored unless the cited
document contains it.

| Source | Gives | Licence / terms | Endpoint | Ingestor |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Destatis GENESIS table 22971 (Statistik untergebrachter wohnungsloser Personen) | official counts of people placed in temporary accommodation, by Land / year and published breakdowns | Data licence Germany – attribution – version 2.0 (dl-de/by-2-0), confirm on the table page at ingest time | https://www-genesis.destatis.de/genesis/online (table 22971) | `counts_law` |
| Federal *Wohnungslosenbericht* (BMWSB / successor) | federal report figures, methods, and caveats | official work; record the exact licence / copyright notice printed in the PDF | BMWSB / destatis publication page current at ingest time | `counts_law` |
| Gesetze im Internet — SGB XII §67 and related | statutory text | federal official-work terms on gesetze-im-internet.de | https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/sgb_12/__67.html | `counts_law` |
| Länder statistical offices and city Wohnungslosenberichte | Land / municipal counts | varies; record each office's licence | per Land / city publication | `counts_law` |
| Bund / Land / municipal contract and grant publications, govdata.de, TED / ELI, Haushaltspläne | public contracts, Zuwendungen, Träger payments | varies (often dl-de/by-2-0 or official work) | per publication | `funding_traeger` |
| Handelsregister / Unternehmensregister, published Impressen and Jahresberichte | Träger identity, published officers / Geschäftsführung | public-register terms; do not scrape behind a paywall or login | per filing | `funding_traeger` |
| Destatis GV-ISys / administrative gazetteer | Land / Kreis / Gemeinde names, AGS codes, population | dl-de/by-2-0 (confirm at ingest time) | Destatis GV-ISys current release | `counts_law` / `places` |
| Bundstagsdrucksachen, Rechnungshof and Land audit reports, court decisions | audits, studies, institutional facts | official work / court publication terms | DIP, Land audit-office sites | `institutions_media` |
| Wikidata, Wikimedia Commons, public broadcaster media pages | institutions, openly licensed media | CC0 / CC-BY-SA / publisher terms, recorded per item | wikidata.org, commons.wikimedia.org | `institutions_media` |

Licences above are the expected starting point, not ingested facts. The ingestor
must copy the licence string from the live source page into `source.license`.
